- **bian-que-perspective**: 扁鹊（秦越人）的思维框架与表达方式。基于《史记·扁鹊仓公列传》及关于“六不治”与“望闻问切”的调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、5条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用扁鹊的视角分析系统诊断、风险预警、故障根因分析与熔断机制。 当用户提到「用扁鹊的视角」「扁鹊会怎么看」「系统诊断模式」「bian que perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我分析一下系统的故障」「如何治未病」「诊断一下目前的问题」也应触发。 ([IchenDEV/superman](https://github.com/IchenDEV/superman/tree/HEAD/skills/bian-que-perspective) · ★16 · 2 copies)

- **biaodian**: 检测并修正中文标点符号。当用户要求校对、检查标点，或提到“标点用法、标点符号、中文标点、校对标点、GB/T 15834”时使用。按 GB/T 15834-2011《标点符号用法》逐处检测，输出“正确用法、错在哪里、官方案例科普”三段式报告，案例取自人民日报、新华社、央视等官媒公开语料。 ([mizzlelover/biaodian](https://github.com/mizzlelover/biaodian/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **biaoge-skill**: 针对 Word（.docx）文档的表格后处理技能。按单元格实际文字动态计算列宽（能单行放下的就单行，放不下的才折行）、自动识别分栏并按单栏宽度适配、统一整套表格样式（6 种黑白灰预设）、可选加表题注、可清理模板页眉页脚残留。当用户提到"表格优化/表格样式/列宽/表格太窄/表格超出栏框/表格折行/分栏文档表格/统一表格风格/Word 表格/调表格"时触发；也应在 pandoc 等工具生成 .docx 之后、作为交付前的收尾工序使用。 ([dawalixi1980/Dawalixi-skill](https://github.com/dawalixi1980/Dawalixi-skill/tree/HEAD/biaoge-skill) · ★1)
- **biaoshu-writer**: 标书撰写器 v5.4.0 - 投标技术标文档自动生成工具。支持解析 txt/pdf/docx/xlsx 招标文件，生成符合评分标准的技术标 Word 文档。适用：技术标编写、交通工程（高速/航道）投标。 ([Jinhong270/AI-Agent](https://github.com/Jinhong270/AI-Agent/tree/HEAD/Xiao-Q/skills/biaoshu-writer-pro) · ★4 · 2 copies)

- **bias**: The Science hand for name the selection effect before the result. You state the hypothesis before the data and report the uncertainty with the estimate. A result without an error bar is a story with a decimal point. Use when the work touches a measurement, when the user says "an experiment", "a measurement", or "a hypothesis", or when they ask "bias", "how do I name the selection effect before the result", or "bias keeps failing". Also use when a measurement must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it unproven. ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/bias))
- **bias**: You are a cognitive psychologist specializing in bias identification and mitigation. Your role is to identify the cognitive biases affecting the situation below, provide specific debiasing strategies for each, and highlight which bias poses the greatest risk. - **bias-audit**: Systematically check a decision or situation for operating psychological biases using Munger's 25 tendencies framework. ([sethmblack/paks-skills](https://github.com/sethmblack/paks-skills/tree/HEAD/bias-audit) · ★10)

- **bias-audit**: Audits *training data* for representativeness and collection bias relative to the operational environment — demographic coverage, geographic/seasonal/temporal gaps, sample-selection bias, labeler bias.
